Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the gr object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 973. The moduli space has dimension at most 347 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 973 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the gr structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 347. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the gr space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 628 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 742. QED.
